data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/PhysicsCanvas.tsx","content":"import { useRef, useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport { CanvasProps, Ball } from '#818cf8', // インディゴ\n  '#f43f5e', // ローズ\n  '#06b6d4', // シアン\n  '#f59e0b', // アンバー\n  '#10b981', // エメラルド\n  '#ec4899', // ピンク\n  '#a855f7', // パープル\n];\n\nexport default function PhysicsCanvas({\n  config,\n  balls,\n  onAddBall,\n  onUpdateBalls,\n}: CanvasProps) {\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n\n  // 物理演算をrequestAnimationFrameループ内で直接参照できるようにRefで最新値を保持\n  const ballsRef = useRef<Ball[]>(balls);\n  const configRef = useRef(config);\n\n  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<DragState>({\n    startX: 0,\n    startY: 0,\n    currentX: 0,\n    currentY: 0,\n    isDragging: false,\n  });\n\n  const dragRef = useRef<DragState>(drag);\n\n  // propsの変更をRefに反映\n  useEffect(() => {\n    ballsRef.current = balls;\n  }, [balls]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    configRef.current = config;\n  }, [config]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    dragRef.current = drag;\n  }, [drag]);\n\n  // 高DPI対応のCanvasリサイズ処理\n  const handleResize = useCallback(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!canvas || !container) return;\n\n    const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n\n    canvas.width = rect.width * dpr;\n    canvas.height = rect.height * dpr;\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (ctx) {\n      ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    handleResize();\n    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {\n      handleResize();\n    });\n    if (containerRef.current) {\n      resizeObserver.observe(containerRef.current);\n    }\n    return () => {\n      resizeObserver.disconnect();\n    };\n  }, [handleResize]);\n\n  // 物理ループ（シミュレーション計算＆描画）\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n\n    const render = () => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      const container = containerRef.current;\n      if (!canvas || !container) {\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (!ctx) {\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const { width, height } = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n      const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n      const currentBalls = [...ballsRef.current];\n\n      // 画面クリア\n      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      // 背景グリッド（薄いガイド線）\n      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03)';\n      ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n      const gridSize = 40;\n      for (let x = gridSize; x < width; x += gridSize) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(x, 0);\n        ctx.lineTo(x, height);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n      for (let y = gridSize; y < height; y += gridSize) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(0, y);\n        ctx.lineTo(width, y);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n\n      // ボール物理更新\n      for (let i = 0; i < currentBalls.length; i++) {\n        const b = currentBalls[i];\n\n        // 重力と空気抵抗\n        b.vy += currentConfig.gravity;\n        b.vx *= 1 - currentConfig.airResistance;\n        b.vy *= 1 - currentConfig.airResistance;\n\n        // 位置の更新\n        b.x += b.vx;\n        b.y += b.vy;\n\n        // 壁との衝突判定（床・天井・左右）\n        // 床\n        if (b.y + b.radius >= height) {\n          b.y = height - b.radius;\n          b.vy = -b.vy * b.bounce;\n          // 微小なバウンス時の振動を停止\n          if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 0.2) b.vy = 0;\n          // 床摩擦\n          b.vx *= 0.98;\n        }\n        // 天井\n        else if (b.y - b.radius <= 0) {\n          b.y = b.radius;\n          b.vy = -b.vy * b.bounce;\n        }\n\n        // 右壁\n        if (b.x + b.radius >= width) {\n          b.x = width - b.radius;\n          b.vx = -b.vx * b.bounce;\n        }\n        // 左壁\n        else if (b.x - b.radius <= 0) {\n          b.x = b.radius;\n          b.vx = -b.vx * b.bounce;\n        }\n\n        // ボール同士の簡易弾性衝突\n        for (let j = i + 1; j < currentBalls.length; j++) {\n          const b2 = currentBalls[j];\n          const dx = b2.x - b.x;\n          const dy = b2.y - b.y;\n          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n          const minDist = b.radius + b2.radius;\n\n          if (dist < minDist && dist > 0) {\n            // 重なりを解消\n            const overlap = (minDist - dist) / 2;\n            const nx = dx / dist;\n            const ny = dy / dist;\n\n            b.x -= nx * overlap;\n            b.y -= ny * overlap;\n            b2.x += nx * overlap;\n            b2.y += ny * overlap;\n\n            // 法線方向の相対速度\n            const kx = b.vx - b2.vx;\n            const ky = b.vy - b2.vy;\n            const p = 2 * (nx * kx + ny * ky) / 2; // 質量比を1:1と仮定\n\n            const restitution = (b.bounce + b2.bounce) / 2;\n            b.vx -= p * nx * restitution;\n            b.vy -= p * ny * restitution;\n            b2.vx += p * nx * restitution;\n            b2.vy += p * ny * restitution;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // ボールの描画\n      for (const b of currentBalls) {\n        ctx.save();\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n\n        // 立体感のあるグラデーション\n        const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(\n          b.x - b.radius * 0.35,\n          b.y - b.radius * 0.35,\n          b.radius * 0.1,\n          b.x,\n          b.y,\n          b.radius\n        );\n        grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff');\n        grad.addColorStop(0.3, b.color);\n        grad.addColorStop(1, '#050b14');\n\n        ctx.fillStyle = grad;\n        ctx.shadowColor = b.color;\n        ctx.shadowBlur = 10;\n        ctx.fill();\n        ctx.restore();\n      }\n\n      // ドラッグ投射ベクトルの描画\n      const currentDrag = dragRef.current;\n      if (currentDrag.isDragging) {\n        const dx = currentDrag.currentX - currentDrag.startX;\n        const dy = currentDrag.currentY - currentDrag.startY;\n        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n\n        if (dist > 5) {\n          ctx.save();\n          // 引っ張る方向と逆（発射方向）\n          const launchX = currentDrag.startX - dx;\n          const launchY = currentDrag.startY - dy;\n\n          // ドラッグ矢印線（スリングショット風）\n          ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);\n          ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.8)';\n          ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n          ctx.beginPath();\n          ctx.moveTo(currentDrag.startX, currentDrag.startY);\n          ctx.lineTo(currentDrag.currentX, currentDrag.currentY);\n          ctx.stroke();\n\n          // 発射軌道予測線\n          ctx.setLineDash([2, 4]);\n          ctx.strokeStyle = '#f43f5e';\n          ctx.beginPath();\n          ctx.moveTo(currentDrag.startX, currentDrag.startY);\n          ctx.lineTo(launchX, launchY);\n          ctx.stroke();\n\n          // 発射始点インジケータ\n          ctx.setLineDash([]);\n          ctx.beginPath();\n          ctx.arc(\n            currentDrag.startX,\n            currentDrag.startY,\n            currentConfig.ballSize,\n            0,\n            Math.PI * 2\n          );\n          ctx.strokeStyle = '#38bdf8';\n          ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n          ctx.stroke();\n          ctx.restore();\n        }\n      }\n\n      ballsRef.current = currentBalls;\n      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n    };\n\n    animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  // 親コンポーネントの状態とも定期同期（不要な高頻度再レンダリングを避けるため500msごと）\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const interval = setInterval(() => {\n      onUpdateBalls(() => ballsRef.current);\n    }, 500);\n    return () => clearInterval(interval);\n  }, [onUpdateBalls]);\n\n  // ポインター操作（マウスドラッグ・スマホタッチ双方対応）\n  const getCoordinates = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    return {\n      x: e.clientX - rect.left,\n      y: e.clientY - rect.top,\n    };\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    const { x, y } = getCoordinates(e);\n    setDrag({\n      startX: x,\n      startY: y,\n      currentX: x,\n      currentY: y,\n      isDragging: true,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    if (!drag.isDragging) return;\n    const { x, y } = getCoordinates(e);\n    setDrag((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      currentX: x,\n      currentY: y,\n    }));\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    if (!drag.isDragging) return;\n\n    const { x, y } = getCoordinates(e);\n    const dx = x - drag.startX;\n    const dy = y - drag.startY;\n    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n\n    // 引っ張った反対方向に初速を付与（スリングショット方式）\n    let vx = -dx * 0.12;\n    let vy = -dy * 0.12;\n\n    // クリック（ドラッグ距離が短い）の場合はランダムな軽い初速で生成\n    if (dist < 8) {\n      vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4;\n      vy = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2;\n    }\n\n    // 初速の上限制限\n    const maxSpeed = 30;\n    const speed = Math.hypot(vx, vy);\n    if (speed > maxSpeed) {\n      vx = (vx / speed) * maxSpeed;\n      vy = (vy / speed) * maxSpeed;\n    }\n\n    const randomColor =\n      BALL_COLORS[Math.floor(Math.random() * BALL_COLORS.length)];\n\n    const newBall: Ball = {\n      id: Date.now() + Math.random(),\n      x: drag.startX,\n      y: drag.startY,\n      vx,\n      vy,\n      radius: config.ballSize,\n      color: randomColor,\n      bounce: config.bounceRate,\n    };\n\n    onAddBall(newBall);\n\n    setDrag({\n      startX: 0,\n      startY: 0,\n      currentX: 0,\n      currentY: 0,\n      isDragging: false,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerCancel = () => {\n    setDrag((prev) => ({ ...prev, isDragging: false }));\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"canvas-wrapper\" config,\n  balls,\n  onAddBall,\n}: CanvasProps) {\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n\n  // 物理演算をrequestAnimationFrameループ内で直接参照できるようにRefで最新値を保持\n  const ballsRef = useRef<Ball[]>(balls);\n  const configRef = useRef(config);\n\n  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<DragState>({\n    startX: 0,\n    startY: 0,\n    currentX: 0,\n    currentY: 0,\n    isDragging: false,\n  });\n\n  const dragRef = useRef<DragState>(drag);\n\n  // propsの変更をRefに反映\n  useEffect(() => {\n    ballsRef.current = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n\n    canvas.width = rect.width * dpr;\n    canvas.height = rect.height * dpr;\n\n    const ctx = containerRef.current;\n      if (!canvas || !container) {\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if configRef.current;\n      const currentBalls = [...ballsRef.current];\n\n      // 画面クリア\n      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      // 背景グリッド（薄いガイド線）\n      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, // 床\n        if (b.y + b.radius >= height) {\n          b.y = height - b.radius;\n          b.vy = -b.vy * b.bounce;\n          // 微小なバウンス時の振動を停止\n          if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 0.2) b.vy = 0;\n          // 床摩擦\n          b.vx *= 0.98;\n        }\n        // 天井\n        else if (b.y - b.radius <= 0) {\n          b.y = b.radius;\n          b.vy = -b.vy * b.bounce;\n        }\n\n        // 右壁\n         b.vx = -b.vx * b.bounce;\n        }\n\n        // ボール同士の弾性衝突（半径に応じた質量比を考慮）\n        for (let j = i + 1; j < currentBalls.length; j++) {\n          const b2 = currentBalls[j];\n          const    // 重なりを解消\n            const overlap = (minDist - dist) / 2;\n            const nx = dx / dist;\n            const ny = dy / dist;\n\n            b.x -= nx * overlap;\n            b.y -= ny * overlap;\n            b2.x += nx * overlap;\n            b2.y += ny * overlap;\n\n            // 相対速度\n            const kx = b.vx - b2.vx;\n            const ky = b.vy - b2.vy;\n            const vn e.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    }\n\n    if (!drag.isDragging) return;\n\n    const { x, y } = getCoordinates(e);\n    const dx = x - drag.startX;\n    const dy = y - './components/Controls';\nimport PhysicsCanvas from './components/PhysicsCanvas';\n\nconst DEFAULT_CONFIG: PhysicsConfig = {\n  gravity: 0.45,\n  bounceRate: 0.82,\n  airResistance: 0.005,\n  ballSize: 18,\n};\n\nconst SAMPLE_COLORS = [\n  '#38bdf8',\n  '#818cf8',\n  '#f43f5e',\n  '#06b6d4',\n  '#f59e0b',\n  '#10b981',\n  '#ec4899',\n];\n\nconst generateBallId = (): string => {\n  if 9)}`;\n};\n\nexport default function App() {\n  const [config, setConfig] = useState<PhysicsConfig>(DEFAULT_CONFIG);\n  const [isPaused, setIsPaused] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // 設定パラメータの更新\n  const handleConfigChange = useCallback(\n    (key: keyof PhysicsConfig, value: number) => {\n      setConfig((prev) => ({\n        ...prev,\n        [key]: value,\n      }));\n\n      // handleUpdateBalls = useCallback(\n    (updater: (prev: Ball[]) => Ball[]) => {\n      setBalls((prev) => updater(prev));\n    },\n    []\n  );\n\n  // ランダムなボールを追加（画面サイズに応じた安全な配置計算）\n  const handleAddRandomBalls = useCallback(\n    (count: number) => {\n      const newBalls: Ball[] = [];\n      const availableWidth =\n        typeof window !== 'undefined'\n          ? window.innerWidth >= 960\n            ? '#818cf8', // インディゴ\n  '#f43f5e', // ローズ\n  '#06b6d4', // シアン\n  '#f59e0b', // アンバー\n  '#10b981', // エメラルド\n  '#ec4899', // ピンク\n  '#a855f7', // パープル\n];\n\nconst generateBallId = (): string => {\n useEffect(() => {\n    isPausedRef.current = isPaused;\n  }, [isPaused]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    dragRef.current = drag;\n  }, [drag]);\n\n  // 高DPI対応のCanvasリサイズ処理\n  const handleResize = ctx.stroke();\n      }\n\n      // 物理演算（一時停止中でない場合のみ更新）\n      if (!paused) {\n        for (let i = 0; i < currentBalls.length; i++) {\n          const b = currentBalls[i];\n\n          // 重力と空気抵抗\n          b.vy += currentConfig.gravity;\n          b.vx *= 1 - currentConfig.airResistance;\n          b.vy *= 1 - currentConfig.airResistance;\n\n          // 位置の更新\n          b.x += b.vx;\n          b.y += b.vy;\n\n          // 壁との衝突判定（床・天井・左右）\n          // 床\n          if (b.y + b.radius >= height) {\n            b.y = height - b.radius;\n            b.vy = -b.vy * b.bounce;\n            // 微小なバウンス時の振動を停止\n            if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 0.2) b.vy = 0;\n            // 床摩擦\n            b.vx *= 0.98;\n          }\n          // 天井\n          else if (b.y - b.radius <= 0) {\n            b.y = b.radius;\n            b.vy = -b.vy * b.bounce;\n          }\n\n          // 右壁\n          if (b.x + b.radius >= width) {\n            b.x = width - b.radius;\n            b.vx = -b.vx * b.bounce;\n   for (let j = i + 1; j < currentBalls.length; j++) {\n            const b2 = currentBalls[j];\n            const dx = b2.x - b.x;\n            const dy = b2.y - b.y;\n            const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n            const minDist = b.radius + b2.radius;\n\n            if (dist < minDist && dist > 0) {\n              // 重なりを解消\n              const overlap = (minDist - dist) / 2;\n              const nx = dx / dist;\n              const ny = dy / dist;\n\n              b.x -= nx * overlap;\n              b.y -= ny * overlap;\n              b2.x += nx * overlap;\n              b2.y += ny * overlap;\n\n              // 相対速度\n              const kx = b.vx - b2.vx;\n              const ky = b.vy - b2.vy;\n              const vn = nx * kx + ny * ky;\n\n              // + b2.bounce) / 2;\n\n                const impulse = ((1 + restitution) * vn) / (1 / m1 + 1 / m2);\n\n                b.vx -= (impulse / m1) * nx;\n                b.vy -= (impulse / m1) * ny;\n    b.x - b.radius * 0.35,\n          b.y - b.radius * 0.35,\n          b.radius * 0.1,\n          b.x,\n          b.y,\n          b.radius\n        );\n        grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff');\n ctx.beginPath();\n          ctx.arc(\n            currentDrag.startX,\n            currentDrag.startY,\n            currentConfig.ballSize,\n            0,\n            Math.PI * 2\n          );\n          ctx.strokeStyle = '#38bdf8';\n          ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n          ctx.stroke();\n          ctx.restore();\n        }\n      }\n\n      ballsRef.current = currentBalls;\n      animationFrameId e.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    }\n\n    if (!drag.isDragging) return;\n\n    const { x, y } = getCoordinates(e);\n    const dx = x - drag.startX;\n    const dy = y -    </aside>\n  );\n}","language":"tsx"},{"path":"src/components/PhysicsCanvas.tsx","content":"import { useRef, useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport { CanvasProps, Ball } from '../types';\n\ninterface DragState {\n  startX: number;\n  startY: number;\n  currentX: number;\n  currentY: number;\n  isDragging: boolean;\n}\n\nconst BALL_COLORS = [\n  '#38bdf8', // スカイブルー\n  '#818cf8', // インディゴ\n  '#f43f5e', // ローズ\n  '#06b6d4', // シアン\n  '#f59e0b', // アンバー\n  '#10b981', // エメラルド\n  '#ec4899', // ピンク\n  '#a855f7', // パープル\n];\n\nconst generateBallId = (): string => {\n  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') {\n    return crypto.randomUUID();\n  }\n  return `ball-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`;\n};\n\nexport default function PhysicsCanvas({\n  config,\n  balls,\n  isPaused = false,\n  onAddBall,\n}: CanvasProps) {\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n\n  // 物理演算をrequestAnimationFrameループ内で直接参照できるようにRefで最新値を保持\n  const ballsRef = useRef<Ball[]>(balls);\n  const configRef = useRef(config);\n  const isPausedRef = useRef(isPaused);\n\n  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<DragState>({\n    startX: 0,\n    startY: 0,\n    currentX: 0,\n    currentY: 0,\n    isDragging: false,\n  });\n\n  const dragRef = useRef<DragState>(drag);\n\n  // propsの変更をRefに反映\n  useEffect(() => {\n    ballsRef.current = balls;\n  }, [balls]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    configRef.current = config;\n  }, [config]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    isPausedRef.current = isPaused;\n  }, [isPaused]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    dragRef.current = drag;\n  }, [drag]);\n\n  // 高DPI対応のCanvasリサイズ処理\n  const handleResize = useCallback(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!canvas || !container) return;\n\n    const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n\n    canvas.width = rect.width * dpr;\n    canvas.height = rect.height * dpr;\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (ctx) {\n      ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    handleResize();\n    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {\n      handleResize();\n    });\n    if (containerRef.current) {\n      resizeObserver.observe(containerRef.current);\n    }\n    return () => {\n      resizeObserver.disconnect();\n    };\n  }, [handleResize]);\n\n  // 物理ループ（シミュレーション計算＆描画）\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n\n    const render = () => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      const container = containerRef.current;\n      if (!canvas || !container) {\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (!ctx) {\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const { width, height } = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n      const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n      const currentBalls = [...ballsRef.current];\n      const paused = isPausedRef.current;\n\n      // 画面クリア\n      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      // 背景グリッド（薄いガイド線）\n      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03)';\n      ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n      const gridSize = 40;\n      for (let x = gridSize; x < width; x += gridSize) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(x, 0);\n        ctx.lineTo(x, height);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n      for (let y = gridSize; y < height; y += gridSize) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(0, y);\n        ctx.lineTo(width, y);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n\n      // 物理演算（一時停止中でない場合のみ更新）\n      if (!paused) {\n        for (let i = 0; i < currentBalls.length; i++) {\n          const b = currentBalls[i];\n\n          // 重力と空気抵抗\n          b.vy += currentConfig.gravity;\n          b.vx *= 1 - currentConfig.airResistance;\n          b.vy *= 1 - currentConfig.airResistance;\n\n          // 位置の更新\n          b.x += b.vx;\n          b.y += b.vy;\n\n          // 壁との衝突判定（床・天井・左右）\n          // 床\n          if (b.y + b.radius >= height) {\n            b.y = height - b.radius;\n            b.vy = -b.vy * b.bounce;\n            // 微小なバウンス時の振動を停止\n            if (Math.abs(b.vy) < 0.2) b.vy = 0;\n            // 床摩擦\n            b.vx *= 0.98;\n          }\n          // 天井\n          else if (b.y - b.radius <= 0) {\n            b.y = b.radius;\n            b.vy = -b.vy * b.bounce;\n          }\n\n          // 右壁\n          if (b.x + b.radius >= width) {\n            b.x = width - b.radius;\n            b.vx = -b.vx * b.bounce;\n          }\n          // 左壁\n          else if (b.x - b.radius <= 0) {\n            b.x = b.radius;\n            b.vx = -b.vx * b.bounce;\n          }\n\n          // ボール同士の弾性衝突（半径に応じた質量比を考慮）\n          for (let j = i + 1; j < currentBalls.length; j++) {\n            const b2 = currentBalls[j];\n            const dx = b2.x - b.x;\n            const dy = b2.y - b.y;\n            const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n            const minDist = b.radius + b2.radius;\n\n            if (dist < minDist && dist > 0) {\n              // 重なりを解消\n              const overlap = (minDist - dist) / 2;\n              const nx = dx / dist;\n              const ny = dy / dist;\n\n              b.x -= nx * overlap;\n              b.y -= ny * overlap;\n              b2.x += nx * overlap;\n              b2.y += ny * overlap;\n\n              // 相対速度\n              const kx = b.vx - b2.vx;\n              const ky = b.vy - b2.vy;\n              const vn = nx * kx + ny * ky;\n\n              // 互いに近づく方向に移動している場合のみ衝突応答\n              if (vn > 0) {\n                const m1 = b.radius * b.radius;\n                const m2 = b2.radius * b2.radius;\n                const restitution = (b.bounce + b2.bounce) / 2;\n\n                const impulse = ((1 + restitution) * vn) / (1 / m1 + 1 / m2);\n\n                b.vx -= (impulse / m1) * nx;\n                b.vy -= (impulse / m1) * ny;\n                b2.vx += (impulse / m2) * nx;\n                b2.vy += (impulse / m2) * ny;\n              }\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // ボールの描画\n      for (const b of currentBalls) {\n        ctx.save();\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n\n        // 立体感のあるグラデーション\n        const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(\n          b.x - b.radius * 0.35,\n          b.y - b.radius * 0.35,\n          b.radius * 0.1,\n          b.x,\n          b.y,\n          b.radius\n        );\n        grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff');\n        grad.addColorStop(0.3, b.color);\n        grad.addColorStop(1, '#050b14');\n\n        ctx.fillStyle = grad;\n        ctx.shadowColor = b.color;\n        ctx.shadowBlur = 10;\n        ctx.fill();\n        ctx.restore();\n      }\n\n      // ドラッグ投射ベクトルの描画\n      const currentDrag = dragRef.current;\n      if (currentDrag.isDragging) {\n        const dx = currentDrag.currentX - currentDrag.startX;\n        const dy = currentDrag.currentY - currentDrag.startY;\n        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n\n        if (dist > 5) {\n          ctx.save();\n          // 引っ張る方向と逆（発射方向）\n          const launchX = currentDrag.startX - dx;\n          const launchY = currentDrag.startY - dy;\n\n          // ドラッグ矢印線（スリングショット風）\n          ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);\n          ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(56,
